################################################
# Collapse of the Soviet Army - Help end the war faster if it's clear that the Soviets no longer stand a chance
################################################
event = {
	id = 2007300
	random = no
	country = SOV

	trigger = {
		event = 2007032 #bitter peace rejected/war resumed
		war = { country = GER }
		war = { country = JAP }
		NOT = { event = 2007033 }
		NOT = { control = { province = 187 data = SOV } } # Leningrad
		NOT = { control = { province = 163 data = SOV } } # Stalingrad
		NOT = { control = { province = 175 data = SOV } } # Moscow
		NOT = { control = { province = 1907 data = SOV } } # Baku
		NOT = { control = { province = 1828 data = SOV } } # Orenburg
		NOT = { control = { province = 1830 data = SOV } } # Kuibyshev/Samara
		NOT = { control = { province = 1372 data = SOV } } # Vladivostok
		NOT = { control = { province = 1410 data = SOV } } # Nikolayevsk
		
		region = { region = "Russia"  country = -2 value = 80 }
	}

	name = "Collapse of the Soviet Army"
	desc = "With the loss of the major urban agglomerations and industrial hubs in central Russia, STAVKA planned to retreat to the Volga and continue resistance, or, failing that, further to the Ural mountains. However, once the enemy breached the Volga river, the supply, logistics, and even morale of the Red army began to rapidly crumble. The infrastructure of Siberia was still very underdeveloped and relied mainly on one major artery which was made unusable by the repeated German bombing. Loss of the oil fields in the Caucasus and the grain-producing regions of Western russia caused severe shortages and deprivation. Meanwhile, the Japanese broke the non-aggression pact and invaded from the East, capturing the main remaining ports used to receive Lend Lease aid. As a result, the Red Army could no longer sustain its operations. The morale of the Soviet soldiers and their faith in victory sharply faltered, and peasants started surrendering to the enemy or deserting the army and going back to their fields. Even Stalin had to reluctantly admit that the organized conventional resistance had became untenable. There was still a plan B - partisan warfare. The vast forests and swamps of Northern Russia appeared to afford such a possibility. But the Russian people, having just suffered a disasterous and demoralizing defeat, had little immediate enthusiasm for further self-sacrifice. They may change their minds, however, once they experience the horrors of the German occupation firsthand."
	style = 2

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 20
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		name = EVT_2400080_NAME
		command = { type = dissent value = 15 }
		command = { type = strength which = all when = 1 where = 0 value = -0.5 }
		
		command = { type = trigger which = 2007325 } #Percentages Agreement/Yalta Conference (Axis Version)
	}
}

event = {
	id = 2007301
	random = no
	country = SOV

	trigger = {
		event = 2007300
		war = { country = GER }
		war = { country = JAP }
		NOT = { control = { province = 187 data = SOV } } # Leningrad
		NOT = { control = { province = 163 data = SOV } } # Stalingrad
		NOT = { control = { province = 175 data = SOV } } # Moscow
		
		lost_national = { value = 70 }
	}

	name = "Anti-Stalin coup"
	desc = "Despite all the purges and thorough vetting of all party, state, and military officials, Stalin could ultimately only delay, but not prevent a coup in the final stages of the war. With the remnants of the Soviet army in total dissaray as the German troops approached the Ural mountains from the West and Japanese and their allies marching from the East, the KGB led by Beria and backed by a number of prominent generals assasinated Stalin and improsoned Molotov. The new government then began negotiations for unconditional surrender with the Axis, hoping to appeal to the more Slavophile elements of the German and Japanese establishment to help preserve at least some semblance of a Russian state in the future."
	style = 2

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 20
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		name = EVT_2400080_NAME
		command = { type = dissent value = -5 }
		command = { type = regime_falls }
		command = { type = domestic which = political_left value = -1 }
		command = { type = headofstate which = 30094 } #Georgy M. Malenkov
		command = { type = headofgovernment which = 30017 } #Beria
		command = { type = foreignminister which = 30020 } #Andrey J. Vyshinsky
		command = { type = armamentminister which = 30089 } #Georgiy K. Zhukov
		command = { type = ministerofsecurity which = 30028 } #Beria
		command = { type = sleepminister which = 30002 } #Stalin
		command = { type = sleepminister which = 30021 } #Molotov
	}
}

#Percentages Agreement warning
event = { 
	id = 2007325
	random = no 
	persistent = yes
	all = yes

	name = "Percentages Agreement"
	desc = "As the Soviet fortunes turned sour, the Axis powers shifted their attention towards the division of the spoils of victory across Eurasia. Germany, as the dominant member of the Axis coalition, naturally showed the least interest in agreeing to the continent's partition into precise 'spheres of influence', preferring instead to leave many questions to be settled once military operations had concluded. Yet even Berlin implicitly recognized that the major Axis powers would each possess their own areas of primary responsibility. Mussolini, by contrast, felt much greater urgency to resolve the question of the post-war order. He watched with growing unease as Japanese armies advanced through Inner Asia towards regions bordering the Middle East and, potentially, Italy's own imperial sphere in East Africa. In particular, he was concerned about the future of the Red Sea and South Arabia, considered vital to Italian supply lines, but now increasingly threatened as the Japanese established contact with the Yemenis and the Saudis. In light of these concerns, Mussolini arranged a meeting between the Axis leaders, where they jotted down an informal 'percentages agreement', delineating their respective spheres of influence across post-war Asia. Japan recognized German/Italian predominance in Africa, Arabia, and Afghanistan west of the Indus river, while Hitler reluctantly promised to respect Japanese interests in India, Mongolia, and China (including Xinjiang). Influence in Soviet Central Asia, where the pan-Turkic nationalists were now a predominant force, was to be divided equally. Meanwhile, separately from the conference, German and Japanese planning staffs finalized proposals for the administration of the occupied Soviet territories, divided roughly along the 70th east meridian, and for the establishment of a number of autonomous and dependent states intended to prevent the re-emergence of a unified Eurasian power. Thus the future political order of Eurasia has been effectively decided well in advance of the Soviet Union's actual collapse."
	style = 2
	
	action = {
		name = "EVT_2400552_ACTA"
		#Allies only
		trigger = { OR = { war = { country = SOV } war = { country = ENG } alliance = { country = JAP } alliance = { country = GER } alliance = { country = ITA } } local_flag = AllInit }
		
		command = { type = relation which = GER value = 5 }
		command = { type = relation which = ITA value = 5 }
		command = { type = relation which = JAP value = 5 }
		command = { }
	}
}

		war = { country = GER }

			control = { province = 56 data = -2 } #Paris is controlled by GER or ally

			NOT = { war = { country = USA country = GER } }

event = {
	id = 2007032
	random = no
	country = SOV

	#decision = {
	#	war = { country = GER }
	#	NOT = { ispuppet = GER }
	#	lost_national = { country = SOV value = 5 }
	#}
	#decision_trigger = {
	#}
	trigger = {
		war = { country = GER }
		NOT = { ispuppet = GER }
		random = 35
		OR = {
			AND = {
				control = { province = 56 data = -2 } # Paris is controlled by an enemy
				lost_national = { country = SOV value = 35 }
				NOT = { land_percentage = { country = GER value = 0.3 } } #SOV has not enough troops
				NOT = { lost_national = { country = GER value = 7 } } #Germany is not losing national provinces
				NOT = { war = { country = GER country = ENG } } #Germany is concentrated on the Soviet front
				NOT = {	war = { country = GER country = VIC } }
				NOT = { war = { country = GER country = USA } }
				NOT = { war = { country = GER country = ITA } }
			}
			AND = {
				NOT = { control = { province = 187 data = SOV } } # Leningrad
				NOT = { control = { province = 163 data = SOV } } # Stalingrad
				NOT = { control = { province = 175 data = SOV } } # Moscow
				OR = {
					NOT = { control = { province = 1907 data = SOV } } # Baku
					NOT = { control = { province = 1828 data = SOV } } # Orenburg
				}
				NOT = { lost_national = { country = GER value = 7 } } #Germany is not losing national provinces
				OR = { #one of the following must be true
					local_flag = SOVMoscow  #Stalin remained in Moscow and was captured
					NOT = { control = { province = 1575 data = SOV } } # Sverdlovsk
					NOT = { control = { province = 1372 data = SOV } } # Vladivostok
					AND = { 
						nuked = -1  #was nuked at least once
						NOT = { nuked = 1 } #SOV never launched nukes
					}
				}
			}
			AND = {
				NOT = { nuked = 1 } #SOV never launched nukes
				lost_national = { country = SOV value = 15 } #SOV is losing ground
				nuked = { country = GER country = SOV data = 1 where = 175 } # Moscow nuked
				OR = {
					nuked = { country = GER country = SOV data = 8 }#SOV nuked 8 times by GER
					AND = {
						nuked = { country = GER country = SOV data = 1 where = 187 } # Leningrad nuked
						nuked = { country = GER country = SOV data = 1 where = 163 } # Stalingrad nuked
						nuked = { country = GER country = SOV data = 1 where = 1907 } # Baku nuked
					}
				}
			}					
		}
	}
	
	name = "EVT_2624_NAME"
	desc = "EVT_2624_DESC"
	style = 2
	picture = "news_paper"
	
	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 10 # Check for trigger conditions every ten days
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }
	
	action = {
		name = EVT_2007032_ACTA
		
		command = { trigger = { war = { country = ENG country = GER }} type = relation which = ENG value = -15 }
		command = { trigger = { war = { country = U01 country = GER }} type = relation which = U01 value = -15 }

event = {
	id = 2007200
	random = no
	country = SOV

	decision = {
		war = { country = GER }
		policy = { country = SOV type = professional_army value = 9 } #Extended mob
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		policy = { country = GER type = professional_army value = 9 } #Extended mob
		NOT = { manpower = 1000 }
		NOT = { lost_national = { country = SOV value = 15 } }
		NOT = { lost_national = { country = GER value = 15 } }
		losses = { country = SOV type = manpower value = 10000000	data = 0 }
		losses = { country = GER type = manpower value = 5000000	data = 0 }
	}
	trigger = {
		war = { country = GER }
		policy = { country = SOV type = professional_army value = 9 } #Extended mob
		policy = { country = GER type = professional_army value = 9 } #Extended mob
		NOT = { lost_national = { country = SOV value = 15 } }
		NOT = { lost_national = { country = GER value = 15 } }
		losses = { country = SOV type = manpower value = 10000000	data = 0 }
		losses = { country = GER type = manpower value = 5000000	data = 0 }
		NOT = { event = 2003181 } #July plot. That means there is a two-front war and Germany is getting close to defeat
	}
	
	name = EVT_2007200_NAME
	desc = EVT_2007200_DESC
	style = 2
	picture = "news_paper"
	
	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 30
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }
	
	action = {
		name = EVT_2007200_ACTA
		command = { trigger = { war = { country = ENG country = GER }} type = relation which = ENG value = -15 }
		command = { trigger = { war = { country = U01 country = GER }} type = relation which = U01 value = -15 }
		command = { trigger = { war = { country = USA country = GER }} type = relation which = USA value = -15 }
		command = { type = event which = 2007201 where = GER when = 15 }
	}
}

	desc = "With the Axis powers now clearly triumphant on the global stage, the prospects of the resistance deteriorated rapidly. The clandestine supply networks that had sustained the insurgency for years collapsed almost overnight as the former Allies either made peace or withdrew from the conflict while the enemy tightened its blockade. At the same time, the enemy was finally free to redeploy large formations from other fronts and launch coordinated anti-partisan campaigns on a scale previously impossible. Entire regions were sealed off, suspected sympathizers faced ruthless reprisals, and whole communities were threatened with extermination should they continue to shelter the resistance.
	More devastating still was the psychological impact of the Allied defeat. With no realistic prospect of outside intervention and victory seemingly beyond reach, many local leaders abandoned armed struggle while an increasingly war-weary population came to regard continued resistance as futile and self-destructive. Desertions multiplied, recruitment dwindled, and collaboration became ever more common. Under such conditions, the maintenance of large partisan armies and so-called 'liberated areas' became impossible, even in the most inaccessible terrain. Before long, the once formidable resistance had been reduced to a scattered underground movement, capable only of isolated raids, sabotage, bombings, and assassinations while waiting for more favorable times to resume the struggle."

		}

			}
		}

			event = 2007033 #bitter peace

				NOT = { control = { province = 175 	data = SOV } } # Moscow
				NOT = { control = { province = 163 	data = SOV } } # Stalingrad
				NOT = { control = { province = 187 	data = SOV } } # Leningrad
			}
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { war = { country = JAP } }
			AND = {
				OR = { 
					NOT = { war = { country = CHI country = JAP } }

				}
				region = { region = "Philippines"	country = -2 value = 70 }
				region = { region = "Indonesia"		country = -2 value = 70 }
			}
		}
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }

	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = GER } } }	type = trigger	which = 2049302 }
	}

		name = ACTINUTILE

		command = { type = dissent value = 5 }
		command = { type = sleepevent which = 2049301 }
		command = { type = sleepevent which = 2049302 }
	}
}

event = {
	id = 2049320
	random = no
	country = USA

	name = EVT_2049320_NAME
	desc = EVT_2049320_DESC
	style = 2
	picture = "news_paper"

	decision = {
		event = 2049300
		NOT = { 
			ispuppet = USA
			alliance = { country = USA country = GER }
			alliance = { country = USA country = JAP }
			alliance = { country = GER country = JAP }
			war 	 = { country = USA country = JAP }
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { atwar = JAP }
			war = { country = GER country = USA }
		}
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		event = { id = 2049300 days = 180 }
	}
	trigger = {
		event = 2049300
		NOT = { 
			ispuppet = USA
			alliance = { country = USA country = GER }
			alliance = { country = USA country = JAP }
			alliance = { country = GER country = JAP }
			war 	 = { country = USA country = JAP }
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { atwar = JAP }
			war = { country = GER country = USA }
		}
		event = { id = 2049300 days = 180 }
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 20
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		name = EVT_2049320_ACTA
		command = { type = domestic				which = political_left value = -1 }
		command = { type = dissent				value =  1 }
		command = { type = belligerence			value =  1 }
		command = { type = relation 			which = GER value = -50 }

		command = { type = event 				which = 2049321 where = JAP when = 1 }
	}
}
